    <!-- Snippet: IeSDh2qNA --><script>
(function () {
  var origPush = window.history.pushState;
  var origReplace = window.history.replaceState;

  function cleanUrl(input) {
    try {
      var url = new URL(input, window.location.origin);
      var changed = false;

      // Strip cid as a top-level param.
      if (url.searchParams.has("cid")) {
        url.searchParams.delete("cid");
        changed = true;
      }
      // Strip cid embedded inside another param's value (malformed URLs).
      url.searchParams.forEach(function (v, k) {
        if (/[?&]cid=/i.test(v)) {
          url.searchParams.set(k, v
            .replace(/[?&]cid=[^&]*/gi, "")
            .replace(/\?+$/, ""));
          changed = true;
        }
      });

      if (!changed) return input;
      var qs = url.searchParams.toString();
      // Preserve relative form if input was relative.
      if (typeof input === "string" && input.charAt(0) === "/") {
        return url.pathname + (qs ? "?" + qs : "") + url.hash;
      }
      return url.toString();
    } catch (e) { return input; }
  }

  // Wrap pushState — fires on every Framer SPA link click. We strip cid
  // from the destination URL so it doesn't propagate to the next page.
  window.history.pushState = function (state, title, url) {
    if (url != null) url = cleanUrl(url);
    return origPush.call(this, state, title, url);
  };

  // Back/forward — if the user navigates back to a URL that contained cid,
  // strip it from what's shown in the bar.
  window.addEventListener("popstate", function () {
    var cleaned = cleanUrl(window.location.href);
    if (cleaned !== window.location.href) {
      origReplace.call(window.history, {}, "", cleaned);
    }
  });

  // We deliberately do NOT touch the URL on initial load — if the user
  // arrives with ?cid=X we leave it so the form / HubSpot can read it.
  // We also do NOT wrap replaceState — the form uses it for its cid flash.
})();
</script><!-- SnippetEnd: IeSDh2qNA --><!-- End of headStart -->

class="framer-oh5i5z" data-framer-name="Content"><div class="framer-1ij8u81" data-framer-name="Content Wrapper" id="blog-content"><div class="framer-z28pd4" data-framer-name="Control every action" data-framer-component-type="RichTextContainer" style="will-change:transform;opacity:0;transform:translateY(12px)"><p dir="auto" class="framer-text framer-styles-preset-1xoowfb">Six months ago, we launched Cogent with a simple thesis: the hardest part of vulnerability management isn't finding the problems. It's fixing them.</p><p dir="auto" class="framer-text framer-styles-preset-1xoowfb">Today, I'm excited to share that we've raised $42 million in Series A funding led by Bain Capital Ventures, with continued support from Greylock Partners and Definition. Founders and executives from OpenAI, Abnormal Security, and Datadog also invested personally. This brings our total funding to $53 million.</p><p dir="auto" class="framer-text framer-styles-preset-1xoowfb">I'm especially proud to welcome <!--$--><a class="framer-text framer-styles-preset-yhd2lm" href="https://www.linkedin.com/in/enriquesalem/" rel>Enrique Salem</a><!--/$--> to our Board of Directors. Enrique helped build and scale some of the defining security companies of the last two decades, including Symantec, Rubrik, and Netskope. He knows what it takes to turn a fast-growing security company into a lasting one, and we're lucky to have him.</p><h2 dir="auto" class="framer-text framer-styles-preset-19faptq">Why We're Here</h2><p dir="auto" class="framer-text framer-styles-preset-1xoowfb">The vulnerability management crisis has gotten worse since we launched. Over 48,000 new CVEs were published in the past year alone, 162 percent more than five years ago. And the window between a vulnerability being disclosed and an attacker exploiting it has collapsed, in part because attackers now use AI to move faster than ever.</p><p dir="auto" class="framer-text framer-styles-preset-1xoowfb">Most security teams already have tools that find these issues. That's not the bottleneck. The bottleneck is everything after discovery: figuring out who owns the affected system, understanding why this specific vulnerability matters in this specific environment, getting the right fix in front of the right engineer, and confirming it actually got done. That work is still manual. It still involves chasing people through Slack threads and outdated spreadsheets. And it still takes days or weeks while attackers move in minutes.</p><p dir="auto" class="framer-text framer-styles-preset-1xoowfb">Security teams don't have a visibility problem. They have an execution problem. That's the gap we built Cogent to close.</p><h2 dir="auto" class="framer-text framer-styles-preset-19faptq">What Cogent Does</h2><p dir="auto" class="framer-text framer-styles-preset-1xoowfb">Our AI agents handle the full lifecycle of vulnerability remediation. They investigate findings in the context of your actual environment, determine who owns what, assess real-world risk, generate remediation steps, and track fixes through to verified completion. Every action is traceable and auditable. Everything runs through configurable approval gates. Organizations deploy autonomous agents without giving up control.</p><p dir="auto" class="framer-text framer-styles-preset-1xoowfb">Customers using Cogent have reduced their exposure windows for critical risks by an average of 97 percent. We're already working with dozens of Fortune 1000 companies and marquee brands across financial services, higher education, retail, and more.</p><h2 dir="auto" class="framer-text framer-styles-preset-19faptq">The Team</h2><p dir="auto" class="framer-text framer-styles-preset-1xoowfb">What I'm most proud of is the team we've assembled.</p><p dir="auto" class="framer-text framer-styles-preset-1xoowfb">Our co-founders lived this problem firsthand. Geng and I came from Abnormal Security, where we helped scale the company from a small startup to a multibillion-dollar business. Thanos led infrastructure at Coinbase, where he ran large-scale vulnerability remediation programs. We've fused security product leaders from Abnormal, Zscaler, and Wiz with world-class AI and software talent from DeepMind, Coinbase, Databricks, Tesla, and Stripe. Our team includes AI researchers who helped develop the transformer architectures behind ChatGPT, Claude, and Gemini.</p><p dir="auto" class="framer-text framer-styles-preset-1xoowfb">It's a group of operators who have built category leaders and want to do it again.</p><h2 dir="auto" class="framer-text framer-styles-preset-19faptq">What's Next</h2><p dir="auto" class="framer-text framer-styles-preset-1xoowfb">This round of funding lets us double down on our core data and AI platforms, scale our go-to-market efforts, and hire exceptional builders. The future of cybersecurity will be agentic, and we're just getting started.</p><p dir="auto" class="framer-text framer-styles-preset-1xoowfb">If you want to see what Cogent can do for your security program, <!--$--><a class="framer-text framer-styles-preset-yhd2lm" href="https://www.cogent.com/book-demo" rel>see the product</a><!--/$--> for yourself. And if you're excited by the idea of building agentic AI from the ground up while making the digital world safer for everyone, we're hiring across R&amp;D and GTM.</p></div></div></section><section
